[codex] /**
[codex]  * The main entry point for the action, called by Github Actions for the step.
[codex]  */
[codex] export async function run(): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     try {
[codex]         setActionId('gradle/actions/dependency-submission')
[codex] 
[codex]         // Configure Gradle environment (Gradle User Home)
[codex]         await setupGradle.setup(new CacheConfig(), new DevelocityConfig(), new WrapperValidationConfig())
[codex] 
[codex]         // Capture the enabled state of dependency-graph
[codex]         const originallyEnabled = process.env['GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED']
[codex] 
[codex]         // Configure the dependency graph submission
[codex]         const config = new DependencyGraphConfig()
[codex]         await dependencyGraph.setup(config)
[codex] 
[codex]         if (config.getDependencyGraphOption() === DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit) {
[codex]             // No execution to perform
[codex]             return
[codex]         }
[codex] 
[codex]         // Only execute if arguments have been provided
[codex]         const executionConfig = new GradleExecutionConfig()
[codex]         const taskList = executionConfig.getDependencyResolutionTask()
[codex]         const additionalArgs = executionConfig.getAdditionalArguments()
[codex]         const executionArgs = `
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.configureondemand=false
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.dependency.verification=off
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.unsafe.isolated-projects=false
[codex]               -Dorg.gradle.isolated-projects=false
[codex]               ${taskList}
[codex]               ${additionalArgs}
[codex]         `
[codex]         const args: string[] = parseArgsStringToArgv(executionArgs)
[codex]         await gradle.provisionAndMaybeExecute(
[codex]             executionConfig.getGradleVersion(),
[codex]             executionConfig.getBuildRootDirectory(),
[codex]             args
[codex]         )
[codex] 
[codex]         await dependencyGraph.complete(config)
[codex] 
[codex]         // Reset the enabled state of dependency graph
[codex]         core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', originallyEnabled)
[codex] 
[codex]         saveDeprecationState()
[codex]     } catch (error) {
[codex]         handleMainActionError(error)
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     // Explicit process.exit() to prevent waiting for hanging promises.
[codex]     await forceExit()
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] run()
[codex] import * as core from '@actions/core'
[codex] import * as github from '@actions/github'
[codex] import * as glob from '@actions/glob'
[codex] import {DefaultArtifactClient} from '@actions/artifact'

[codex] 
[codex] const DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX = 'dependency-graph_'
[codex] 
[codex] export async function setup(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     const option = config.getDependencyGraphOption()
[codex]     if (option === DependencyGraphOption.Disabled) {
[codex]         core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', 'false')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex]     // Download and submit early, for compatability with dependency review.
[codex]     if (option === DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit) {
[codex]         maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR', config.getReportDirectory())
[codex]         await downloadAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config)
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     core.info('Enabling dependency graph generation')
[codex]     core.exportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED', 'true')
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_CONTINUE_ON_FAILURE', config.getDependencyGraphContinueOnFailure())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR', config.getJobCorrelator())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_ID', github.context.runId.toString())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REF', github.context.ref)
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_SHA', getShaFromContext())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_WORKSPACE', getWorkspaceDirectory())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR', config.getReportDirectory())
[codex] 
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_PROJECTS', config.getExcludeProjects())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_PROJECTS', config.getIncludeProjects())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_EXCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS', config.getExcludeConfigurations())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_INCLUDE_CONFIGURATIONS', config.getIncludeConfigurations())
[codex] 
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_URL', config.getPluginRepository().getUrl())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_USERNAME', config.getPluginRepository().getUsername())
[codex]     maybeExportVariable('GRADLE_PLUGIN_REPOSITORY_PASSWORD', config.getPluginRepository().getPassword())
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function maybeExportVariable(variableName: string, value: string | boolean | undefined): void {
[codex]     if (!process.env[variableName]) {
[codex]         if (value !== undefined) {
[codex]             core.exportVariable(variableName, value)
[codex]         }
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] export async function complete(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     const option = config.getDependencyGraphOption()
[codex]     try {
[codex]         switch (option) {
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.Disabled:
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.Generate: // Performed via init-script: nothing to do here
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit: // Performed in setup
[codex]                 return
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateAndSubmit:
[codex]                 await findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config, false)
[codex]                 return
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateSubmitAndUpload:
[codex]                 await findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config, true)
[codex]                 return
[codex]             case DependencyGraphOption.GenerateAndUpload:
[codex]                 await findAndUploadDependencyGraphs(config)
[codex]         }
[codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex]         warnOrFail(config, option, e)
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function downloadAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     try {
[codex]         await submitDependencyGraphs(await downloadDependencyGraphs(config))
[codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex]         warnOrFail(config, DependencyGraphOption.DownloadAndSubmit, e)
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function findAndSubmitDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig, uploadAfterSubmit: boolean): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     const dependencyGraphFiles = await findDependencyGraphFiles()
[codex]     try {
[codex]         await submitDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles)
[codex]     } catch (e) {
[codex]         try {
[codex]             await uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles, config)
[codex]         } catch (uploadError) {
[codex]             core.info(String(uploadError))
[codex]         }
[codex]         throw e
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     if (uploadAfterSubmit) {
[codex]         await uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles, config)
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function findAndUploadDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     if (isRunningInActEnvironment()) {
[codex]         core.info('Dependency graph not supported in the ACT environment.')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     await uploadDependencyGraphs(await findDependencyGraphFiles(), config)
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function downloadDependencyGraphs(config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;string[]> {
[codex]     const findBy = github.context.payload.workflow_run
[codex]         ? {
[codex]               token: getGithubToken(),
[codex]               workflowRunId: github.context.payload.workflow_run.id,
[codex]               repositoryName: github.context.repo.repo,
[codex]               repositoryOwner: github.context.repo.owner
[codex]           }
[codex]         : undefined
[codex] 
[codex]     const artifactClient = new DefaultArtifactClient()
[codex] 
[codex]     let dependencyGraphArtifacts = (
[codex]         await artifactClient.listArtifacts({
[codex]             latest: true,
[codex]             findBy
[codex]         })
[codex]     ).artifacts.filter(artifact => artifact.name.startsWith(DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX))
[codex] 
[codex]     const artifactName = config.getDownloadArtifactName()
[codex]     if (artifactName) {
[codex]         core.info(`Filtering for artifacts ending with ${artifactName}`)
[codex]         dependencyGraphArtifacts = dependencyGraphArtifacts.filter(artifact => artifact.name.includes(artifactName))
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     for (const artifact of dependencyGraphArtifacts) {
[codex]         const downloadedArtifact = await artifactClient.downloadArtifact(artifact.id, {
[codex]             findBy
[codex]         })
[codex]         core.info(`Downloading dependency-graph artifact ${artifact.name} to ${downloadedArtifact.downloadPath}`)
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     return findDependencyGraphFiles()
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function findDependencyGraphFiles(): Promise&lt;string[]> {
[codex]     const globber = await glob.create(`${getReportDirectory()}/**/*.json`)
[codex]     const allFiles = await globber.glob()
[codex]     const unprocessedFiles = allFiles.filter(file => !isProcessed(file))
[codex]     unprocessedFiles.forEach(markProcessed)
[codex]     core.info(`Found dependency graph files: ${unprocessedFiles.join(', ')}`)
[codex]     return unprocessedFiles
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function uploadDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles: string[], config: DependencyGraphConfig): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     if (dependencyGraphFiles.length === 0) {
[codex]         core.info('No dependency graph files found to upload.')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     const workspaceDirectory = getWorkspaceDirectory()
[codex] 
[codex]     const artifactClient = new DefaultArtifactClient()
[codex]     for (const dependencyGraphFile of dependencyGraphFiles) {
[codex]         const relativePath = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(dependencyGraphFile)
[codex]         core.info(`Uploading dependency graph file: ${relativePath}`)
[codex]         const artifactName = `${DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_PREFIX}${path.basename(dependencyGraphFile)}`
[codex]         await artifactClient.uploadArtifact(artifactName, [dependencyGraphFile], workspaceDirectory, {
[codex]             retentionDays: config.getArtifactRetentionDays()
[codex]         })
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function submitDependencyGraphs(dependencyGraphFiles: string[]): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     if (dependencyGraphFiles.length === 0) {
[codex]         core.info('No dependency graph files found to submit.')
[codex]         return
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     for (const dependencyGraphFile of dependencyGraphFiles) {
[codex]         try {
[codex]             await submitDependencyGraphFile(dependencyGraphFile)
[codex]         } catch (error) {
[codex]             if (error instanceof Error && error.name === 'HttpError') {
[codex]                 error.message = translateErrorMessage(dependencyGraphFile, error)
[codex]             }
[codex]             throw error
[codex]         }
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function translateErrorMessage(jsonFile: string, error: Error): string {
[codex]     const relativeJsonFile = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(jsonFile)
[codex]     const mainWarning = `Dependency submission failed for ${relativeJsonFile}.\n${error.message}`
[codex]     if (error.message === 'Resource not accessible by integration') {
[codex]         return `${mainWarning}
[codex] Please ensure that the 'contents: write' permission is available for the workflow job.
[codex] Note that this permission is never available for a 'pull_request' trigger from a repository fork.
[codex]         `
[codex]     }
[codex]     return mainWarning
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] async function submitDependencyGraphFile(jsonFile: string): Promise&lt;void> {
[codex]     const octokit = getOctokit()
[codex]     const jsonContent = fs.readFileSync(jsonFile, 'utf8')
[codex] 
[codex]     const jsonObject = JSON.parse(jsonContent)
[codex]     jsonObject.owner = github.context.repo.owner
[codex]     jsonObject.repo = github.context.repo.repo
[codex]     const response = await octokit.request('POST /repos/{owner}/{repo}/dependency-graph/snapshots', jsonObject)
[codex] 
[codex]     const relativeJsonFile = getRelativePathFromWorkspace(jsonFile)
[codex]     core.notice(`Submitted ${relativeJsonFile}: ${response.data.message}`)
[codex] }
[codex] function getReportDirectory(): string {
[codex]     return process.env.DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR!
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function isProcessed(dependencyGraphFile: string): boolean {
[codex]     const markerFile = `${dependencyGraphFile}.processed`
[codex]     return fs.existsSync(markerFile)
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function markProcessed(dependencyGraphFile: string): void {
[codex]     const markerFile = `${dependencyGraphFile}.processed`
[codex]     fs.writeFileSync(markerFile, '')
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function warnOrFail(config: DependencyGraphConfig, option: String, error: unknown): void {
[codex]     if (!config.getDependencyGraphContinueOnFailure()) {
[codex]         throw new JobFailure(error)
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     core.warning(`Failed to ${option} dependency graph. Will continue.\n${String(error)}`)
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function getOctokit(): InstanceType&lt;typeof GitHub> {
[codex]     return github.getOctokit(getGithubToken())
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function getRelativePathFromWorkspace(file: string): string {
[codex]     const workspaceDirectory = getWorkspaceDirectory()
[codex]     return path.relative(workspaceDirectory, file)
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function getShaFromContext(): string {
[codex]     const context = github.context
[codex]     const pullRequestEvents = [
[codex]         'pull_request',
[codex]         'pull_request_comment',
[codex]         'pull_request_review',
[codex]         'pull_request_review_comment'
[codex]         // Note that pull_request_target is omitted here.
[codex]         // That event runs in the context of the base commit of the PR,
[codex]         // so the snapshot should not be associated with the head commit.
[codex]     ]
[codex]     if (pullRequestEvents.includes(context.eventName)) {
[codex]         const pr = (context.payload as PullRequestEvent).pull_request
[codex]         return pr.head.sha
[codex]     } else {
[codex]         return context.sha
[codex]     }
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] function isRunningInActEnvironment(): boolean {
[codex]     return process.env.ACT !== undefined
[codex] }
[codex] import org.gradle.util.GradleVersion
[codex] 
[codex] // Only run when dependency graph is explicitly enabled
[codex] if (getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_ENABLED') != "true") {
[codex]   return
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] // Do not run for unsupported versions of Gradle
[codex] def gradleVersion = GradleVersion.current().baseVersion
[codex] if (gradleVersion &lt; GradleVersion.version("5.2") ||
[codex]     (gradleVersion >= GradleVersion.version("7.0") && gradleVersion &lt; GradleVersion.version("7.1"))) {
[codex]   if (getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_CONTINUE_ON_FAILURE') != "true") {
[codex]     throw new GradleException("Dependency Graph is not supported for ${gradleVersion}. No dependency snapshot will be generated.")
[codex]   }
[codex]   logger.warn("::warning::Dependency Graph is not supported for ${gradleVersion}. No dependency snapshot will be generated.")
[codex]   return
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] // Attempt to find a unique job correlator to use based on the environment variable
[codex] // This is only required for top-level builds
[codex] def isTopLevelBuild = gradle.getParent() == null
[codex] if (isTopLevelBuild) {
[codex]   def reportFile = getUniqueReportFile(getVariable('GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR'))
[codex] 
[codex]   if (reportFile == null) {
[codex]     logger.warn("::warning::No dependency snapshot generated for step. Could not determine unique job correlator - specify GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR var for this step.")
[codex]     return
[codex]   }
[codex] 
[codex]   logger.lifecycle("Generating dependency graph into '${reportFile}'")
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] apply from: 'gradle-actions.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin-apply.groovy'
[codex] 
[codex] /**
[codex]  * Using the supplied jobCorrelator value:
[codex]  * - Checks if report file already exists
[codex]  * - If so, tries to find a unique value that does not yet have a corresponding report file.
[codex]  * - When found, this value is set as a System property override.
[codex]  */
[codex] File getUniqueReportFile(String jobCorrelator) {
[codex]     def reportDir = getVariable('DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR')
[codex]     def reportFile = new File(reportDir, jobCorrelator + ".json")
[codex]     if (!reportFile.exists()) return reportFile
[codex] 
[codex]     // Try at most 100 suffixes
[codex]     for (int i = 1; i &lt; 100; i++) {
[codex]         def candidateCorrelator = jobCorrelator + "-" + i
[codex]         def candidateFile = new File(reportDir, candidateCorrelator + ".json")
[codex]         if (!candidateFile.exists()) {
[codex]            System.properties['GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_JOB_CORRELATOR'] = candidateCorrelator
[codex]            return candidateFile
[codex]         }
[codex]     }
[codex] 
[codex]     // Could not determine unique job correlator
[codex]     return null
[codex] }
[codex] 
[codex] /**
[codex] * Return the environment variable value, or equivalent system property (if set)
[codex] */
[codex] String getVariable(String name) {
[codex]   return System.properties[name] ?: System.getenv(name)
[codex] }

[codex] blocks broader activation; it is not a reason to omit dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local validation and dry run
[codex] 
[codex] The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] `renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] `renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] 
[codex] Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] [`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] not in a second desired-version inventory.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Exact-image security evidence
[codex] 
[codex] `exact-image-security-evidence.yml` runs weekly and by manual dispatch. It has
[codex] no pull-request trigger and findings do not create a merge gate. The job fails
[codex] when rendering, registry resolution, database download, package inventory, or a
[codex] vulnerability scan is incomplete; vulnerability findings themselves leave the
[codex] scan successful. The final upload runs even after failure and retains evidence
[codex] for seven days without relying on SARIF hosting or a paid service.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow calls `scripts/security/render-image-scan-inputs.sh`, which uses
[codex] the production Kustomize overlays, controller chart versions from
[codex] `deploy/scripts/lib/version-contract.sh`, their checked-in values, and the
[codex] Prometheus/Kiali post-renderers. It includes Helm-created controllers and hooks,
[codex] operator-supplied cert-manager solver and Prometheus config-reloader images,
[codex] standalone Jaeger and infrastructure images, and the Tilt Temurin, Kind, and
[codex] frontend production-smoke images. First-party service images remain under the
[codex] release workflow and are excluded from this scanner job.
[codex] 
[codex] Production/controller images are resolved and scanned as `linux/arm64`.
[codex] sed: can't read /workspace/orchestration/.github/workflows/renovate-config-validation.yml: No such file or directory
[codex]    ownership, activation procedure and failure triage. Transfer active operational
[codex]    ownership there in `docs/OWNERSHIP.md`; link from README and relevant CI docs.
[codex] 2. Add the shared preset and root config. Explicitly configure Kubernetes and
[codex]    Helm-values file patterns; Dockerfile detection alone is insufficient.
[codex] 3. Add documented Renovate annotations/extraction for chart pins in shell and
[codex]    Tilt, image strings in Tilt/scripts, Kind node images, and pinned tool releases.
[codex]    Bind each chart to its real chart name/repository, not just a GitHub project.
[codex]    Cover repeated pins without duplicate native/custom extraction.
[codex] 4. Preserve pinning exceptions, production artifact ownership and checksum
[codex]    contracts. Make stateful/platform/checksum-coupled proposals approval-gated.

[codex] 
[codex] Use current documented schema options. Validate registry tag formats, K3s build
[codex] suffixes, `v` prefixes and image compatibility suffixes. Do not treat Pod Security
[codex] labels as dependency versions. Existing docs and version-contract comments that
[codex] describe intentional holds should become review prerequisites, not silent ignores.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Confirm `node --version` reports major version 24, then run
[codex] `renovate-config-validator` against both files. Validate config against the local
[codex] preset before it is published, then use Renovate's local platform with debug
[codex] extraction/lookup output. Local mode does not implement branch-file mutation and
[codex] downgrades `full` dry runs to lookup; record GitHub-authenticated lookups and a true
[codex] GitHub-platform full dry run as explicit Phase 12 gaps rather than giving an agent a
[codex] token or blocking configuration preparation. Treat Renovate exceptions or error
[codex] logs as failures even if its exit status is zero. Run `bash -n` and `shellcheck` on
[codex] annotated shell files, actionlint on changed workflows, and the existing static
[codex] guardrails when their documented prerequisites are present. Missing local
[codex] prerequisites are blockers, not reasons to bypass checks.
[codex]

[codex] 
[codex] Every orchestration row in the coverage contract has extracted identities and
[codex] lookup results or an explicit gap. Config validates and checked-in dependency
[codex] selections remain unchanged. A successful schema check alone is insufficient.
[codex] Publication, authenticated GitHub lookup, published-preset resolution, and hosted
[codex] branch mutation simulation are Phase 12 acceptance work and do not block Phase 1.

[codex] # Dependency Automation Coverage
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Orchestration extraction and exact-image workflow prepared;
[codex] hosted observation and cross-repository scanner phases remain pending.
[codex] 
[codex] **Last updated:** 2026-09-06
[codex] 
[codex] This report records observed extraction and lookup behavior. It is not a list
[codex] of desired dependency versions. The preserved
[codex] [dependency review](dependency-update-review-2026-09-06.md) remains the
[codex] historical acceptance benchmark, and
[codex] [Dependency Automation](../dependency-automation.md) owns operating policy.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Phase 1 pilot evidence
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate `44.65.5` was initially run under Node `24.13.0` and rerun under the
[codex] workspace baseline Node `24.20.0` against a plain copy of the checkout. The
[codex] unpublished shared preset and repository configuration were merged in memory,
[codex] repository config discovery was suppressed, and local platform dry-run mode
[codex] was used with debug logging. This avoids resolving the not-yet-published GitHub
[codex] preset and includes newly created, untracked files.
[codex] 
[codex] The final extraction rerun found 98 occurrences in 34 package files:
[codex] 
[codex] | Manager | Files | Occurrences | Evidence |
[codex] | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
[codex] | Dockerfile | 1 | 1 | `nginx/Dockerfile.prod-smoke-assets` -> `alpine` |
[codex] | GitHub Actions | 3 | 11 | Both existing workflows plus the new validator workflow; checkout, setup-node, Node input, and runner identities were recognized. |
[codex] | Helm values | 2 | 13 | Kyverno digest overrides and five kube-prometheus-stack image identities were recognized. |
[codex] | Kubernetes | 11 | 29 | Stateful infrastructure, Jaeger, service init images, gateway images, local first-party images, and Kubernetes API kinds were recognized. |
[codex] | Regex | 17 | 44 | Shell/Tilt chart pins, tool releases, Kind, Kiali, inline Temurin, probe images, Playwright, and the Renovate validator were recognized. |
[codex] 
[codex] Three of the GitHub Actions occurrences come from the manual hosted-dry-run job
[codex] and repeat already-covered runner, setup-node, and Node identities; they do not
[codex] add a new dependency surface.
[codex] 
[codex] The first lookup run completed in about 34 seconds and reported 57 distinct
[codex] dependency records, 31 outdated records, and 104 candidate branches before
[codex] routine limits and dashboard approvals. Live lookups succeeded for all six
[codex] configured Helm repositories, npm, and most container registries. The final
[codex] rerun completed lookup and returned 101 candidate branches, with the expected
[codex] missing-GitHub-token warning and an anonymous Docker token warning. Registry
[codex] lookup failures are retained below instead of being presented as a clean scan.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate's documented local platform supports `extract` and `lookup`, not a
[codex] true `full` dry run. Passing `--dry-run=full` falls back to lookup behavior.
[codex] Therefore extraction and live lookup are demonstrated locally, but branch-file
[codex] mutation and the published preset resolution still require a no-write hosted
[codex] observation after publication. No GitHub token was available in this worker,
[codex] by design, so GitHub-backed identities were extracted with
[codex] `github-token-required` and were not looked up. Those lookups are deferred to
[codex] the user-triggered Phase 12 job, which receives its ephemeral read-only token
[codex] inside GitHub Actions.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Phase 2 exact-image evidence
[codex] 
[codex] After excluding generated `tmp/` output, a Node 24 extraction rerun found 104
[codex] occurrences across 35 package files. The new workflow contributed six native
[codex] GitHub Actions records: checkout, pinned yq, pinned Trivy setup, pinned Trivy
[codex] CLI input, artifact upload, and the runner identity. The native manager owns all
[codex] of them; no scanner-specific custom manager was needed.
[codex] 
[codex] The offline render and YAML extraction path produced 32 distinct
[codex] image/platform targets from checked-in sources: 28 production/controller refs
[codex] for `linux/arm64`, three local-only refs for `linux/amd64`, and the Istio gateway
[codex] chart's unresolved `image: auto` marker. The workflow scans each registry ref
[codex] only after resolving the platform child digest. The `auto` marker is explicitly
[codex] unscannable without the live Istio injector, so the workflow retains it as a
[codex] known gap without turning that expected limitation into a scan error. Unexpected
[codex] render, resolution, database, and Trivy failures do fail. The render included
[codex] controller hooks, the cert-manager solver argument, the Prometheus
[codex] config-reloader argument, and all five explicit monitoring image overrides.
[codex] 
[codex] Representative scans used Trivy `0.74.0` with vulnerability DB version 2,
[codex] updated `2026-09-06 07:00:11 UTC`. These observed artifact digests are evidence
[codex] for this run, not desired-version inputs:
[codex] 
[codex] | Baseline item | Exact platform artifact and inventory evidence | Advisory comparison |
[codex] | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] | Redis | The checked-in index selected `redis@sha256:d6550289...` for ARM64. Trivy identified Alpine 3.21.7 and 17 OS packages, but did not identify the Redis server binary/version; the only Go binary inventory was `gosu`. | `GHSA-c8h9-259x-jff4` is **missing** because the affected Redis application was not inventoried. The scan cannot prove Redis 7.4.8 or remediation. |
[codex] | Tilt Temurin base | The checked-in index selected `eclipse-temurin@sha256:609c3d51...` for AMD64. Trivy identified Alpine 3.23.4 and 44 OS packages but no OpenJDK/Temurin/JDK package or application component. | The review's JDK 25.0.3+9 observation is **missing** from scanner evidence. A successful Alpine scan does not establish the Java CPU level. |
[codex] | External Secrets | The rendered mutable tag resolved through index `sha256:876e627d...` to ARM64 artifact `ghcr.io/external-secrets/external-secrets@sha256:c2649798...`. Trivy found Debian 13.4, five OS packages, and 377 Go packages, but the primary module had an empty version. | `GHSA-r2pg-r6h7-crf3` and `GHSA-wv26-88m5-6h59` are **missing**. The unversioned primary module prevents a supported affected-version match. |
[codex] | Kyverno | The checked-in index selected `reg.kyverno.io/kyverno/kyverno@sha256:875beb24...` for ARM64. Trivy found the primary Go module as `v1.18.0+dirty`. | `GHSA-79gf-7frw-68m9` is **reproduced** as `CVE-2026-54523`; the GHSA URL is retained in the finding references and the fixed version is 1.18.2. |
[codex] | Prometheus | The checked-in index selected `quay.io/prometheus/prometheus@sha256:65acbbb6...` for ARM64. The image exposed no OS metadata, but Trivy inventoried the Go module as 3.11.1. | Stored XSS `GHSA-vffh-x6r8-xx99` is **reproduced** as `CVE-2026-40179`. Remote-read DoS `GHSA-8rm2-7qqf-34qm` is **missing** from this scan. |
[codex] 
[codex] The missing rows remain acceptance gaps, not clean results or false positives.
[codex] Raw inventories, vulnerability JSON, resolver manifests, stderr, and database
[codex] metadata are the authoritative workflow artifacts. Job summaries deliberately
[codex] separate OS-package and application-package counts and identify mutable source
[codex] refs; they do not infer embedded versions from tags.
[codex]

[codex] 4794-[codex] ## Local validation and dry run
[codex] 4795-[codex] 
[codex] 4796-[codex] The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] 4797:[codex] `renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] 4798:[codex] same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] 4799-[codex] `renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] 4800-[codex] repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] 4801-[codex] Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] 4802:[codex] platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] 4803-[codex] perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] 4804-[codex] full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] 4805-[codex] job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] 4806-[codex] 
[codex] 4807:[codex] Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] 4808-[codex] [`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] 4809-[codex] not in a second desired-version inventory.

[codex] --
[codex] 4814-[codex]   warnings and migrations as failures; correct the preset or repository config.
[codex] 4815-[codex] - **No extraction:** check the owning file's native manager pattern or adjacent
[codex] 4816-[codex]   annotation and compare debug logs. Do not seed the historical expected version
[codex] 4817-[codex]   into configuration.
[codex] 4818:[codex] - **No lookup or wrong package:** verify the real datasource identity, chart
[codex] 4819-[codex]   name, registry URL, tag prefix, build suffix, and flavor suffix. Record an
[codex] 4820-[codex]   unresolved standard-tool gap instead of adding a custom crawler.
[codex] 4821-[codex] - **Authentication or resolution failure:** preserve the failure. Fix encrypted
[codex] 4822-[codex]   Maven/registry access in the supported trust boundary; do not use Maven Local,

[codex] 4848-[codex] ## Pilot evidence
[codex] 4849-[codex] 
[codex] 4850-[codex] Renovate `44.65.5` was run under Node `24.13.0` against a plain copy of the
[codex] 4851:[codex] checkout. The unpublished shared preset and repository configuration were
[codex] 4852-[codex] merged in memory, repository config discovery was suppressed, and local
[codex] 4853-[codex] platform dry-run mode was used with debug logging. This avoids resolving the
[codex] 4854-[codex] not-yet-published GitHub preset and includes newly created, untracked files.
[codex] 4855-[codex] 
[codex] --
[codex] 4866-[codex] These are the recorded initial-pilot totals. The subsequently added manual
[codex] 4867-[codex] hosted-dry-run job repeats the already-covered `actions/setup-node` identity; it
[codex] 4868-[codex] does not add a new dependency surface.
[codex] 4869-[codex] 
[codex] 4870:[codex] The first lookup run completed in about 34 seconds and reported 57 distinct
[codex] 4871-[codex] dependency records, 31 outdated records, and 104 candidate branches before
[codex] 4872:[codex] routine limits and dashboard approvals. Live lookups succeeded for all six
[codex] 4873-[codex] configured Helm repositories, npm, and most container registries. A repeat run
[codex] 4874-[codex] was interrupted by anonymous registry timeouts; that failure is retained below
[codex] 4875-[codex] instead of being presented as a clean scan.
[codex] 4876-[codex] 
[codex] 4877:[codex] Renovate's documented local platform supports `extract` and `lookup`, not a
[codex] 4878:[codex] true `full` dry run. Passing `--dry-run=full` falls back to lookup behavior.
[codex] 4879:[codex] Therefore extraction and live lookup are demonstrated locally, but branch-file
[codex] 4880-[codex] mutation and the published preset resolution still require a no-write hosted
[codex] 4881-[codex] observation after publication. No GitHub token was available in this worker,
[codex] 4882-[codex] by design, so GitHub-backed identities were extracted with
[codex] 4883:[codex] `github-token-required` and were not looked up. Those lookups are deferred to
[codex] 4884-[codex] the user-triggered Phase 12 job, which receives its ephemeral read-only token
[codex] 4885-[codex] inside GitHub Actions.
[codex] 4886-[codex]

Kube-state-metrics, Prometheus, and Operator lookups produced later lines; Grafana and config-reloader had registry timeouts on repeat validation. The chart's nonstandard `sha` fields are not associated with tags by the native manager, so complete tag-plus-digest mutation is an explicit **gap** and must fail review until handled as a companion change. |

[codex] 4902-[codex] | Production controller overrides: `deploy/helm-values/{cert-manager,external-secrets,kyverno}.values.yaml` | Seven Kyverno image repositories/digests; cert-manager and External Secrets are chart-only | Kyverno's digest-only structure was extracted but lacks a trustworthy tag and must not be refreshed from `latest`; all chart-value image proposals are approval-gated. [codex] 29-
[codex] 30-| Item | Finding |
[codex] 31-| --- | --- |
[codex] 32-| Checked-in reference | `redis:7-alpine@sha256:7aec734b...` |
[codex] 33-| Version resolved from the digest | Redis 7.4.8, image created 2026-04-17 |
[codex] 34-| Recommended supported-line patch | Redis 7.4.11 |
[codex] --
[codex] 39-issue that may lead to remote code execution. This has practical relevance to
[codex] 40-the current ACL model: `session-gateway` and `redis-ops` receive `+@all`, while
[codex] 41-the other service identities are substantially narrower.
[codex] 42-
[codex] 43-Recommendation: refresh the Redis 7.4 image to 7.4.11 before considering a
[codex] 44-Redis 8 migration. Review whether `session-gateway` needs `+@all` or can use an
[codex] 45-explicit command allowlist that excludes administrative commands such as
[codex] 46-`RESTORE`.

[codex] 51:### Spring Boot-Managed Backend Stack
[codex] 52-
[codex] 53:The checked-in Spring Boot 3.5.14 BOM resolves these notable components:
[codex] 54-
[codex] 55-| Component | Boot 3.5.14 | Boot 3.5.16 | Security floor observed in this review |
[codex] 56-| --- | --- | --- | --- |
[codex] 57-| Spring Framework | 6.2.18 | 6.2.19 | 6.2.19 |
[codex] 58-| Spring Security | 6.5.10 | 6.5.11 | No advisory found for 6.5.10 in the queried database |
[codex] 59-| Jackson BOM | 2.21.2 | 2.21.4 | 2.21.4 |
[codex] 60-| Netty | 4.1.132.Final | 4.1.135.Final | 4.1.137.Final |
[codex] 61-| Embedded Tomcat | 10.1.54 | 10.1.55 | 10.1.58 |
[codex] 62-
[codex] 63-Findings include:
[codex] 64-
[codex] 65-- Spring Web 6.2.18 is affected by an `UriComponentsBuilder` SSRF advisory;
[codex] 66-  6.2.19 contains the fix.
[codex] 67-- Jackson 2.21.2 is affected by deserialization authorization and polymorphic
[codex] 68-  type validation issues; the relevant 2.x fixes are present in 2.21.4.
[codex] 69-- Netty 4.1.132 is affected by request-smuggling, decompression denial of
[codex] 70-  service, CORS bypass, CRLF injection, and resource exhaustion advisories.
[codex] 71-- Tomcat 10.1.54 is in the affected ranges for several authorization and
[codex] 72-  authentication advisories. The newest reviewed fixes require 10.1.58.
[codex] 73-
[codex] 74:Recommendation: first move the coordinated Java repositories to Spring Boot
[codex] 75-3.5.16. Because its BOM still selects Netty 4.1.135 and Tomcat 10.1.55, assess
[codex] 76-temporary managed-version overrides to Netty 4.1.137 and Tomcat 10.1.58. Keep
[codex] 77:this patch batch separate from the Spring Boot 4 migration.
[codex] 78-
[codex] 79-Applicability caveat: several Tomcat findings concern container-managed
[codex] 80-FORM/DIGEST authentication, WebDAV, AJP, or direct HTTP/2 behavior that the
[codex] 81-current services do not appear to use. The affected components are still in
[codex] 82-the deployed classpath, so patching remains preferable to relying only on
[codex] 83-configuration non-use. Netty is directly relevant to the reactive Session
[codex] 84-Gateway and other WebFlux/WebClient paths.
[codex] 85-
[codex] 86-Representative advisories:
[codex] 87-
[codex] 88-- [Spring Framework SSRF](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-7m2p-62gw-p8qq)
[codex] 89-- [Tomcat security constraint bypass](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-5m62-pw8w-7w9f)
[codex] --
[codex] 242-The pinned `postgres:16-alpine` digest resolves to PostgreSQL 16.13. The
[codex] 243-current patch at review time is 16.15.
[codex] 244-
[codex] 245-Recommendation: refresh to PostgreSQL 16.15 as routine security and bug-fix
[codex] 246-maintenance. PostgreSQL 16 remains supported until November 2028, so the
[codex] 247-existence of PostgreSQL 18 does not make a database major-version migration
[codex] 248-urgent.

[codex] 250-## Unsupported Or Approaching-EOL Migration Batches
[codex] 251-
[codex] 252-### Spring 4 Migration
[codex] 253-
[codex] 254:Spring Boot 3.5.16 was the latest 3.5 release found, and the standard 3.5
[codex] 255-open-source lifecycle ended in June 2026. Current Spring releases at review
[codex] 256:time were Spring Boot 4.1.1, Spring Cloud 2025.1.3, and Spring Security 7.1.1.
[codex] 257-
[codex] 258:Recommendation: complete the Spring Boot 3.5 security patch batch first, then
[codex] 259:plan the already identified coupled migration to Spring Boot 4, Spring Cloud
[codex] 260:2025.1, Spring Framework 7, Spring Security 7, Spring Modulith 2, SpringDoc 3,
[codex] 261:Testcontainers 2, ShedLock 7, and JUnit Platform 6.
[codex] 262-
[codex] 263-### Node.js
[codex] 264-
[codex] 265-The frontend Dockerfiles use Node 20, which reached end of life on 2026-04-30.
[codex] 266-The devcontainer already selects Node 22 even though the orchestration docs
[codex] 267-still say Node 20.
[codex] 268-
[codex] 269-Recommendation: move production and development frontend builds to Node 24
[codex] 270-LTS. Do not target Node 26 until it enters LTS unless there is a deliberate
[codex] 271-reason to follow the current feature line.
[codex] 272-
[codex] 273-Source: [Node.js releases](https://nodejs.org/en/about/previous-releases).
[codex] --
[codex] 283-amd64 archive and should be made architecture-aware as part of that work.

[codex] 291-time.
[codex] 292-

[codex] 296-evidence.

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] cd \""'${validation_tmp}/repo"
[codex] set +e
[codex] RENOVATE_CONFIG="${validation_config}" LOG_LEVEL=debug LOG_FORMAT=json npx --yes --package=renovate@44.65.5 renovate --platform=local --dry-run=lookup --onboarding=false --require-config=ignored --config-file-names=renovate-unpublished-validation.json > "${validation_tmp}/renovate.log" 2>&1

[codex] if [[ "${renovate_status}" -ne 0 ]]; then
[codex]   exit "${renovate_status}"
[codex] fi
[codex] if jq -e '"'select(.level >= 40 or (.msg | test(\"uncaught|unhandled|fatal\"; \"i\")))' \""'${validation_tmp}/renovate.log" >/dev/null; then
[codex]   echo '"'Renovate emitted an error, fatal message, or uncaught/unhandled exception.' >&2
[codex]   exit 91
[codex] fi" in /workspace/service-common

[codex] +++ b/docs/dependency-automation.md
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,94 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex]   exit 91
[codex] fi" in /workspace/service-common

[codex] +++ b/docs/dependency-automation.md
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,94 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex]   echo '"'Renovate emitted an error, fatal message, or uncaught/unhandled exception.' >&2
[codex]   exit 91
[codex] fi" in /workspace/service-common

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,94 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,94 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `service-common` integration and review checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, and GitHub Actions managers discover this repository's
[codex] +version catalog, build scripts, wrapper distribution, and workflow actions.
[codex] +There are currently no Dockerfiles in this repository. Add file-specific
[codex] +manager configuration only if a future dependency declaration is not covered
[codex] +by a native manager.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. It does not turn every
[codex] +version inherited from the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, or Spring Modulith BOMs
[codex] +into an independently updateable declaration. The resolved dependency graph
[codex] +described below is the security inventory for those inherited versions.
[codex] +
[codex] +Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +In particular, Spring Boot 4 and the related Spring Cloud, Spring Framework,
[codex] +Spring Security, Spring Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, ShedLock, and test
[codex] +platform lines are a compatibility migration, not a routine patch. Renovate
[codex] +cannot select the correct release-train combination or coordinate consumer
[codex] +services by itself.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. Its job token receives only `contents: write`, which is
[codex] +required by GitHub's Dependency Submission API. The graph is submitted
[codex] +directly and is not retained as a workflow artifact or published as a Build
[codex] +Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The action's default resolution task visits all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations. Do not add project or configuration filters without proving
[codex] +equivalent coverage: the platform modules contribute imported BOMs, while the
[codex] +library modules contribute compile, runtime, and test dependency trees.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 3 validation generated an official plugin snapshot without
[codex] +submitting it. The snapshot contained 216 resolved coordinate/version entries
[codex] +across the four subprojects. It included the Spring Boot, Spring Cloud, and
[codex] +Spring Modulith BOM coordinates and these inherited component families where
[codex] +the current build actually resolves them:
[codex] +
[codex] +| Component family | Resolution evidence |
[codex] +| --- | --- |
[codex] +| Spring Framework | Library runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +| Spring Security | `service-core` and `service-web` test classpaths; compile-only APIs are also resolved through compile classpaths |
[codex] +| Embedded Tomcat | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the servlet stack |
[codex] +| Netty | `service-web` test runtime, which exercises the reactive stack |
[codex] +| Jackson | `service-core` and `service-web` runtime and test classpaths |
[codex] +
[codex] +The entry count and resolved versions will change as the checked-in dependency
[codex] +selection changes; they are evidence from onboarding, not a desired-version
[codex] +inventory. A successful local snapshot proves generation and transitive
[codex] +coverage, but only a hosted run after repository activation proves submission
[codex] +and creates Dependabot alert input.
[codex] +
[codex] +GitHub's dependency graph reports resolved packages but does not decide which
[codex] +BOM or override should remediate an inherited finding. A Dependabot alert is
[codex] +also not proof that an available Spring Boot update fixes every Framework,
[codex] +Security, Jackson, Tomcat, or Netty advisory. Trace the affected configuration
[codex] +with Gradle dependency insight and review any override as a service-owner
[codex] +decision.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +For every Renovate pull request:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Keep the shared preset's no-automerge and dashboard-approval behavior.
[codex] +2. Review the resolved dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring
[codex] +   release-train compatibility, and whether an inherited advisory is actually
[codex] +   remediated.
[codex] +3. Run the repository-required validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +   ```bash
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +   ./gradlew clean build
[codex] +   ```
[codex] +
[codex] +4. For platform or shared-library changes, validate all consuming services
[codex] +   before release as required by
[codex] +   [versioning and compatibility](versioning-and-compatibility.md). Do not
[codex] +   publish artifacts or update consumers from an unreviewed bot branch.
[codex] +
[codex] +Graph generation failures are failures, not clean security results. Preserve
[codex] +dependency-resolution and submission errors for triage; do not substitute
[codex] +Maven Local, omit configurations, or enable a hosted Build Scan to make the job
[codex] +pass.

[codex] +documented in
[codex] +[docs/dependency-automation.md](docs/dependency-automation.md). Dependency graph
[codex] +submission inventories BOM-managed transitive packages for Dependabot alerts;
[codex] +it does not publish service-common artifacts.
[codex] +
[codex]  `service-common`'s own version and backwards-compatibility contract lives in
[codex]  [docs/versioning-and-compatibility.md](docs/versioning-and-compatibility.md).
